# Seven boundary points: the complete AH/DE side-line class

Consider the single-exterior placement of N7_OP_LINE_CAPS.md. If the
line joining the two heptagon-interior points crosses the literal hull
edges AH and DE, the minimum ordinary triangle area is strictly below
93/2000<A*. This excludes the entire edge-pair class at every height.

The proof has fifty-six fixed cubic rows. They contain **19,348 printed
positive integer terms**. Every factor and every determinant sign is
defined below. The optional standard-library checker reads the printed
manuscript. No search, optimizer, coordinate sample or unprinted
certificate is a premise, and no square factors are used.

All areas and determinants below are doubled. Write s=93/1000 and
K=149/20<Gamma2=4+2sqrt(3), and suppose every actual triangle has unsigned
area at least s.

## 1. The literal line class and its containing quadrilateral

Use the strict convex actual octagon

    A=(0,a), B=(b,0), C=(c,0), D=(1,d),
    E=(1,e), F=(f,1), G=(g,z), H=(0,h)

and heptagon-interior O=(x,y), P=(u,v), where

    0<a<h<1, 0<b<c<1, 0<d<e<1,
    b<=f<=c, d<=h, a<=e, 0<g<f, h<z<1.

G is strictly above HF. All cyclic octagon triangles are positive.
Orient OP from its intersection with AH toward its intersection with
DE, and label O before P in that direction. Thus U=u-x>0 and

    EOP,FOP,GOP,HOP>0; AOP,BOP,COP,DOP<0.          (1)

The segment between the edge intersections lies in the actual
quadrilateral ADEH. Hence both O,P are strictly inside ADEH. The two
same-sign arcs are E,F,G,H and A,B,C,D. If I,J occur in that order
in either arc, then IJO,IJP>0: the cap between I,J lies in one open
half-plane of OP and the segment of OP lies across its endpoint chord.

The exact identity

    IJP-IJO=IOP-JOP

will propagate hull-chord signs. Write V=v-y. The actual OP clearances
give four useful inequalities:

    (h-a)U=HOP-AOP>=2s,
    (e-d)U=EOP-DOP>=2s,
    (e-a)U-V=EOP-AOP>=2s,
    (h-d)U+V=HOP-DOP>=2s.                          (2)

No comparison between h and e, or between y and v, is assumed.

The next table is a complete binary sign tree. Each row lists its
literal path from the root, beginning with the AFO cut. Whenever two
rows share a path up to a branching triangle, both strict signs of that
triangle occur among its descendants. The optional checker verifies
this assertion directly, without using the discovery search. Every
split is an actual triangle, so a zero sign is excluded by its area
clearance. There are fifty-six leaves.

The following finite rules produce the additional signs for each row.

1. Every cyclic octagon triangle is positive. Every edge test of the
   containing heptagon ABCDEFH and quadrilateral ADEH is positive at
   O,P. Include all same-sign-arc chord signs from Section 1.
2. A four-consecutive-vertex cap has capacity one. If its cyclic
   endpoint chord is IJ, its cap is on the negative side of IJ. If
   IOP<0<JOP, then IJO>0; if IOP>0>JOP, then IJP>0.
   Otherwise monotonicity along OP would place both points in that cap.
3. For every hull chord IJ, IJP-IJO=IOP-JOP propagates a known sign in
   its indicated increasing or decreasing direction.
4. Three positive edge tests put a point in an actual hull triangle.
   Any chord half-plane containing that triangle contains the point,
   by strict barycentric interpolation.
5. Every consecutive triple, every consecutive four-vertex cap, and
   each of AEF,BEF,ABE,ABF,CDG,CGH,CDH,DGH has capacity one. If one
   point is inside and all but one edge tests for the other are
   positive, the remaining edge test is negative.
6. If both O,P lie in ABEF or CDGH, each diagonal separates them:
   its two actual component triangles are among the eight long
   triangles, of area less than 69/100<Gamma2*s. The identity in
   Section 1 determines which point is on which side whenever the
   two diagonal endpoints have opposite OP signs.
7. For T=O,P the exact identities

       z BHT=h BGT-y_T[b(z-h)+hg],
       DFT=(1-d)CFT+[1-c+d(c-f)](1-y_T),
       f AGT=g AFT-x_T AFG,
       (1-b)CET=(1-c)BET+(c-b)e(1-x_T)

   give, respectively, BGT<0 => BHT<0, CFT>0 => DFT>0,
   AFT<0 => AGT<0, and BET>0 => CET>0. Every coefficient used
   here has the indicated positive sign from the normalized domain.

## 3. Two explicitly justified rational boxes

Put

    eta=93/790, ell=7347/69700, Fmax=1-ell,
    h0=4s/Fmax, h1=1-s, f0=4s/(1-eta), x0=s/(h1-eta),
    ht0=257/500, ht1=203/250, ft=Ks/(1-eta),
    xt=s/(ht1-eta), et=eta+s/(1-ft).

The ordinary single-exterior bounds give Box W. If a row proves
O inside AGH, use the stronger Box T. The latter follows from the
occupied-ear calculation already established in
N7_THREE_FIVE_UPPER_HEIGHT.md Section 3. For clarity, its relevant
inequalities are recalled here. AGH>=3s and FGH>=s give

    Fmax*(h-eta)*(1-h)-3s*(1-h)-s*(h-eta)>=0.

The exact concave-quadratic calculation gives ht0<h<ht1.
The paired-ear Cauchy bound raises f(1-a) to at least Gamma2*s>Ks;
therefore f>=ft and a<=1-Ks. Actual CDE clearance and c>=f give
e>=eta+s/(1-ft)=et. The AGH fan gives g>=3xt and

    g-x>=2xt.                                      (3)

The box in either case closes strict endpoints and incorporates
U>=2x0, respectively U>=2xt, from (2). The constants x0,xt exceed ell,
so the other bound U>=2ell is weaker for coordinate endpoints.
The exact coordinate order is (a,b,c,d,e,f,g,h,z,x,y,u,v).

| Coordinate | W lower | W upper | T lower | T upper |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| a | eta | 1-4s | eta | 1-Ks |
| b | x0 | Fmax | xt | Fmax |
| c | ell | Fmax | ft | Fmax |
| d | eta | h1 | eta | ht1 |
| e | h0 | 1 | et | 1 |
| f | f0 | Fmax | ft | Fmax |
| g | x0 | 3/5 | 3xt | 3/5 |
| h | h0 | h1 | ht0 | ht1 |
| z | h0+s | 1 | ht0+s | 1 |
| x | x0 | Fmax-2x0 | xt | Fmax-2xt |
| y | eta | 1-s | eta | 1-s |
| u | 3x0 | Fmax | 3xt | Fmax |
| v | eta | 1-s | eta | 1-s |

The baseline estimates are proved in N7_OP_LINE_CAPS.md; g<3/5 is
proved in N7_LONG_TRIANGLE_CAPS.md. The ordinate upper bounds follow
from FHT,EFT>=s and

    (1-e)FHT+(1-h)EFT=EFH(1-y_T),
    EFH=(1-f)(1-h)+f(1-e)<=2-e-h.

selected lower endpoint and L_(2j+1) its selected upper endpoint
minus the coordinate. Write t=x0,hmax=h1 in Box W and

|---:|---|---:|---|
| 26 | h-a-s | 27 | 697/790-h+a |
| 28 | e-d-s | 29 | 697/790-e+d |

    L40=(1-eta)U-V-2s,
    L41=(hmax-eta)U+V-2s.

In Box T append

    L40=g-x-2xt,
    L41=(1-eta)U-V-2s,
    L42=(hmax-eta)U+V-2s.

The first two inequalities in (2), h-a<=hmax-eta and
e-d<=697/790 justify L38,L39. For the last two factors, use the last
two inequalities in (2), e-a<=1-eta, h-d<=hmax-eta and U>0.
The extra T factor is (3). Every affine factor is nonnegative.

## 4. Support, cap, occupancy and five-chain factors

The following factors retain their already proved definitions:

    EP=f(1-a)-4s, EL=hc-4s, ER=e(1-b)-4s,
    SUP=f^2(z-a)-fg(1-a)-4s(f-g),
    P_AHGFE=f(1-e)+(1-f)(1-a-11s),
    P_BCDEF=e(1-f)+(1-e)(1-b-11s),
    P_DEFGH=g(h-d)+z-h-11sg,
    P_HABCD=(h-11s)(1-c)+dc,
    P_ABCDE=(e-11s)b+a(1-b),
    UBR=9s-e(1-b),
    UTR=9s(f-g)-(1-g)[(1-d)(f-g)+(1-z)(1-f)],
    PAIR_BL=hc-ABC-ABH-2s,
    PAIR_BR=e(1-b)-BCD-CDE-2s.

The endpoint supports, paired-ear estimates and upper corner containers
are proved in N7_OP_LINE_CAPS.md and N7_LONG_TRIANGLE_CAPS.md. For
example sqrt(ABC)+sqrt(ABH)<=sqrt(hc), together with ABC,ABH>=s,
gives PAIR_BL>=0. The right-side proof is identical.

For W=ABCD,CDEF,EFGH,GHAB, UW means 8s-area(W).
For T=AEF,BEF,ABE,ABF,CDG,CGH,CDH,DGH, UT means 69/100-T.
For T=ABC,ABH,BCD,CDE,AGH,FGH, EAR_T means 121/250-T.
All are nonnegative by the published metric cap and long-triangle bounds.

A triangle factor CT_1 or CT_2 means area(T)-3s or area(T)-Ks.
Its indicated one or two actual occupants are listed below. For an
actual convex quadrilateral containing O,P, QW means area(W)-8s.
If both diagonals also separate O,P, RW_IJ means

    area(W)-(29/5)s-epsilon*(IJO-IJP),                (6)

where epsilon is the sign of IJO. This is the threshold-free spread
bound, since 29/5<3+2sqrt(2). The factor SW_IJ instead uses 6 in place
of 29/5, and is used only for W=ABEF or CDGH, whose area is at most one.
Indeed area(ABEF)=1-(e-a)(f-b)<=1 and
area(CDGH)=z-(c-g)(h-d)<=1.

The new notation Q1_W means area(W)-(24/5)s. Here W is any positively
ordered actual convex quadrilateral, possibly with O or P as a vertex,
and it contains the other actual point identified in the table.
To justify the cost, let its four fan areas around that point be
a0,b0,c0,d0. Every fan area is at least s, and the determinant identity
for the two actual diagonal triangles gives
|a0*c0-b0*d0|>=s^2. After interchanging the products,
a0*c0>=b0*d0+s^2>=2s^2. Therefore

    area(W)>=2sqrt(a0*c0)+2sqrt(b0*d0)
            >=2(1+sqrt(2))s>(24/5)s.

This is the one-interior-point quadrilateral proof recorded in
U3_FOUR_ONE_SIDE.md, Section 3.

For five actual hull vertices A0,B0,C0,D0,E0 in cyclic order, put

    X=A0B0E0, Y=A0D0E0, Z0=A0B0D0.

The universal five-chain support argument of
N7_THREE_FIVE_UPPER_HEIGHT.md proves XY>=11s(X-Z0). If X-Z0<=0
this is immediate. Otherwise the supporting rays A0B0 and E0D0
meet beyond both contacts, and their endpoint triangle has area
XY/(X-Z0)>11s. If X<=B or Y<=B, respectively, this gives the
nonnegative quadratic factors

    F5_A0B0C0D0E0_X = B*Y-11s*(X-Z0),
    F5_A0B0C0D0E0_Y = 11s*Z0-(11s-B)*X.             (7)

Every used B is in the next table. It follows by containment in one of
the four-consecutive caps, an ear triangle, or a long triangle. The
available upper bounds are 8s for even-starting caps
ABCD,CDEF,EFGH,GHAB; (1-s)^2 for HABC,FGHA; 9s for BCDE,DEFG;
69/100 for the eight long triangles; 121/250 for the six ears above;
and 1-4s for DEF,EFG. Taking the minimum of applicable bounds is valid.

For ELK or ERK, a listed occupant in ABC or ABH, respectively BCD or
CDE, raises that ear's area to at least 3s. The paired-ear Cauchy
inequality gives hc>=Gamma2*s or e(1-b)>=Gamma2*s, hence the factor
ELK=hc-Ks or ERK=e(1-b)-Ks. SUPK is SUP with 4 replaced by K,
under the corresponding listed occupation of AGH or FGH.

All conditional premises used by the printed sums are recorded here.
Triangle entries list occupants; Q entries contain O,P; Q1 entries
list their single occupant. For every R or S entry both actual
diagonals separate O,P, and the suffix selects the diagonal in (6).

## 5. Exact contradictions and complete scope

Each row's printed polynomial W is a positive integer sum of products
of the stated nonnegative factors. Thus W>=0. Expand it as
sum(c_alpha X^alpha). The selected positive rational box gives the exact
upper bound

    B(W)=sum_(c_alpha>0)c_alpha product_j(upper_j^alpha_j)
         +sum_(c_alpha<0)c_alpha product_j(lower_j^alpha_j).

Every value in the next table is strictly negative. The fifty-six rows
form the complete binary sign tree in Section 2. This contradiction
proves the entire literal AH/DE edge-pair class has an actual triangle
with doubled area below s.

This statement does not rotate the missing top contact or identify
distinct seven-contact normalizations. The other edge-pair classes
and the all-interior N7 placement remain separate obligations.
